Missing name raises ImproperlyConfigured.
registry.py[RG8L]: F:Defines the Apps registry that populates app configs and models in three phases and tracks swappable and lazy model operations; creates the global apps. | R:code:django/apps/config.py,code:django/core/exceptions.py,code:django/conf/__init__.py | A:Apps, apps | S:populate() is thread-locked, idempotent, raises RuntimeError on reentry. get_models and get_swappable_settings_name use class-level functools.cache (shared across Apps instances); call clear_cache() after mutation. register_model must not import; same module+name re-register only warns, else RuntimeError. unset_installed_apps restores configs without repopulating; set/unset must pair.

===/root/aoci-eval/repos-r5/django/django/conf/===
__init__.py[RC8M]: F:Implements lazy settings: LazySettings proxy, Settings loader for DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE, UserSettingsHolder, and the global settings object. | R:code:django/conf/global_settings.py,code:django/core/exceptions.py,code:django/utils/functional.py,code:django/__init__.py | A:settings, LazySettings, Settings, UserSettingsHolder, ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLE, SettingsReference | S:Only UPPERCASE names are settings. Values cached in __dict__, cleared when _wrapped set. Relative MEDIA_URL/STATIC_URL get script prefix once, cached. Empty SECRET_KEY raises. Settings() sets os.environ TZ, calls time.tzset(); ValueError if TIME_ZONE absent from /usr/share/zoneinfo. INSTALLED_APPS, ALLOWED_HOSTS etc. must be list/tuple. configure() twice: RuntimeError; lowercase option: TypeError.
global_settings.py[FC7L]: F:Holds Django's default values for every setting, which project settings modules override. | R:code:django/conf/__init__.py | A:- | S:Must not import django.utils.translation (circular); uses local gettext_noop. MANAGERS aliases ADMINS. SECRET_KEY defaults empty, failing on first access. Only uppercase names are settings.
